```yaml
                              - row [ref=f2e500]:
                                - rowheader "Dermatology" [ref=f2e501]
                                - cell "9%" [ref=f2e502]
                                - cell "9.4%" [ref=f2e503]
                        - paragraph [ref=f2e504]: "General Medicine takes the largest share because it is the front door: roughly one in four of those visits is referred onward the same day."
                    - generic [ref=f2e505]:
                      - generic [ref=f2e506]:
                        - generic [ref=f2e507]:
                          - progressbar "Patient satisfaction" [ref=f2e508]:
                            - generic: 98%
                          - heading "98% patient satisfaction" [level=3] [ref=f2e512]
                          - paragraph [ref=f2e513]: From 11,400 post-visit surveys. We publish the complaints too, in the annual report.
                        - generic [ref=f2e514]:
                          - progressbar "On-time consultations" [ref=f2e515]:
                            - generic: 96%
                          - heading "96% on-time consultations" [level=3] [ref=f2e519]
                          - paragraph [ref=f2e520]: Measured as starting within ten minutes of the booked slot. The missing 4% is mostly emergencies pulling a consultant away.
                      - paragraph [ref=f2e521]: Two ProgressRings, one BarChart and one PieChart — all four ship inside the same zero-dependency package as the rest of this page.
                - region "What patients say" [ref=f2e522]:
                  - generic [ref=f2e523]:
                    - generic [ref=f2e524]:
                      - generic [ref=f2e525]: Patient stories
                      - heading "What people actually say afterwards" [level=2] [ref=f2e527]
                      - paragraph [ref=f2e528]: Collected from post-visit surveys. Names changed, wording theirs.
                    - list [ref=f2e529]:
                      - listitem [ref=f2e530]:
                        - figure "Priya Sharma Priya Sharma Paediatrics · Indiranagar" [ref=f2e532]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f2e533]: I booked at 11pm on a Sunday with a toddler asleep on me and had a paediatric slot for 9am Monday. No phone queue, no callback that never came.
                          - generic [ref=f2e534]:
                            - img "Priya Sharma" [ref=f2e536]
                            - generic [ref=f2e537]:
                              - generic [ref=f2e538]: Priya Sharma
                              - generic [ref=f2e539]: Paediatrics · Indiranagar
                      - listitem [ref=f2e540]:
                        - figure "Arun Menon Arun Menon Orthopaedics · Whitefield" [ref=f2e542]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f2e543]: Dr. Desai talked me out of surgery and onto twelve weeks of physiotherapy. He was right. I have not thought about my knee since March.
                          - generic [ref=f2e544]:
                            - img "Arun Menon" [ref=f2e546]
                            - generic [ref=f2e547]:
                              - generic [ref=f2e548]: Arun Menon
                              - generic [ref=f2e549]: Orthopaedics · Whitefield
                      - listitem [ref=f2e550]:
                        - figure "Fatima Sheikh Fatima Sheikh General Medicine · Koramangala" [ref=f2e552]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f2e553]: The confirmation SMS arrived before I had closed the tab, and the reminder came the evening before. Small things, but they are the ones clinics usually get wrong.
                          - generic [ref=f2e554]:
                            - img "Fatima Sheikh" [ref=f2e556]
                            - generic [ref=f2e557]:
                              - generic [ref=f2e558]: Fatima Sheikh
                              - generic [ref=f2e559]: General Medicine · Koramangala
                      - listitem [ref=f2e560]:
                        - figure "Rahul Kulkarni Rahul Kulkarni Cardiology · HSR Layout" [ref=f2e562]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f2e563]: Nineteen minutes from walking in to walking out with a prescription, and that included the ECG. I have waited longer for coffee.
                          - generic [ref=f2e564]:
                            - img "Rahul Kulkarni" [ref=f2e566]
                            - generic [ref=f2e567]:
                              - generic [ref=f2e568]: Rahul Kulkarni
                              - generic [ref=f2e569]: Cardiology · HSR Layout
                      - listitem [ref=f2e570]:
                        - figure "Ananya Ghosh Ananya Ghosh Dermatology · Jayanagar" [ref=f2e572]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f2e573]: Rescheduling took four taps. I have had airlines make that harder, and they had my money already.
                          - generic [ref=f2e574]:
                            - img "Ananya Ghosh" [ref=f2e576]
                            - generic [ref=f2e577]:
                              - generic [ref=f2e578]: Ananya Ghosh
                              - generic [ref=f2e579]: Dermatology · Jayanagar
                      - listitem [ref=f2e580]:
                        - figure "Joseph Thomas Joseph Thomas Neurology · Hebbal" [ref=f2e582]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f2e583]: They sent my scan report to my GP without me having to ask twice. That is the entire review.
                          - generic [ref=f2e584]:
                            - img "Joseph Thomas" [ref=f2e586]
                            - generic [ref=f2e587]:
                              - generic [ref=f2e588]: Joseph Thomas

                      - group [ref=f2e597]:
                        - heading "Do I need a referral to book?" [level=3] [ref=f2e599] [cursor=pointer]
                        - paragraph [ref=f2e601]: No. You can book any specialist at MediCare Plus directly, without a referral from a GP. A referral letter is still useful if you have one, because it saves the consultant re-taking a history you have already given once.

                    - list [ref=f3e963]:
                      - listitem [ref=f3e964]:
                        - figure "Rhian Morgan Photographer, Cardiff" [ref=f3e966]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f3e967]: I bought the Transit pack for one trip and it has been my only bag for a year. The suspended laptop sleeve alone justifies it.
                          - generic [ref=f3e969]:
                            - generic [ref=f3e970]: Rhian Morgan
                            - generic [ref=f3e971]: Photographer, Cardiff
                      - listitem [ref=f3e972]:
                        - figure "Aditya Menon Architect, Bengaluru" [ref=f3e974]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f3e975]: Ordered the Meridian 38 expecting to send it back. Three months on it is the only watch I wear — it actually fits a small wrist.
                          - generic [ref=f3e977]:
                            - generic [ref=f3e978]: Aditya Menon
                            - generic [ref=f3e979]: Architect, Bengaluru

                        - generic [ref=f4e640]: Verdict
                        - generic [ref=f4e641]: "Sweet spot: May–September — barely any rain, the terraces are still green from the wet season, and the reef at Amed is glass-flat."
                    - paragraph [ref=f4e643]:
                      - text: Both charts are VivekUI components —
                      - code [ref=f4e644]: LineChart
                      - text: and
                      - code [ref=f4e645]: BarChart
                      - text: — rendered as inline SVG on the server. No chart library is installed in this project.
                  - link "See the full Bali trip" [ref=f4e647] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: /destinations/bali#best-time
                - region "What people say when they get back" [ref=f4e648]:
                  - generic [ref=f4e649]:
                    - generic [ref=f4e650]:
                      - generic [ref=f4e651]: Travellers
                      - heading "What people say when they get back" [level=2] [ref=f4e653]
                    - list [ref=f4e654]:
                      - listitem [ref=f4e655]:
                        - figure "Nadia Okonkwo Nadia Okonkwo Bali, September" [ref=f4e657]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f4e658]: The Batur climb started at two in the morning and I complained the entire way up. Standing on the rim at sunrise I understood exactly why they do it that way.
                          - generic [ref=f4e659]:
                            - img "Nadia Okonkwo" [ref=f4e661]
                            - generic [ref=f4e662]:
                              - generic [ref=f4e663]: Nadia Okonkwo
                              - generic [ref=f4e664]: Bali, September
                      - listitem [ref=f4e665]:
                        - figure "Tom Aldridge Tom Aldridge Kyoto, November" [ref=f4e667]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f4e668]: Booked Kyoto for November purely off the rainfall chart on the trip page. Eight days, not one wet afternoon. No other operator gave me the numbers.
                          - generic [ref=f4e669]:
                            - img "Tom Aldridge" [ref=f4e671]
                            - generic [ref=f4e672]:
                              - generic [ref=f4e673]: Tom Aldridge
                              - generic [ref=f4e674]: Kyoto, November
                      - listitem [ref=f4e675]:
                        - figure "Priya Ramnath Priya Ramnath Ladakh, July" [ref=f4e677]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f4e678]: Three days of doing almost nothing in Leh before we went anywhere high. Two people on another tour we met at Pangong were both ill. We were fine.
                          - generic [ref=f4e679]:
                            - img "Priya Ramnath" [ref=f4e681]
                            - generic [ref=f4e682]:
                              - generic [ref=f4e683]: Priya Ramnath
                              - generic [ref=f4e684]: Ladakh, July
                      - listitem [ref=f4e685]:
                        - figure "Lukas Brenner Lukas Brenner Swiss Alps, August" [ref=f4e687]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f4e688]: Nine hours over the Sefinenfurgge was the hardest day I have had on a walking holiday, and the guide never once made me feel like the slow one.
                          - generic [ref=f4e689]:
                            - img "Lukas Brenner" [ref=f4e691]
                            - generic [ref=f4e692]:
                              - generic [ref=f4e693]: Lukas Brenner
                              - generic [ref=f4e694]: Swiss Alps, August
                      - listitem [ref=f4e695]:
                        - figure "Maria Kalliopi Maria Kalliopi Santorini, June" [ref=f4e697]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f4e698]: Six of us on Santorini in June. We walked the caldera at half six in the morning and had it entirely to ourselves. By ten it was heaving.
                          - generic [ref=f4e699]:
                            - img "Maria Kalliopi" [ref=f4e701]
                            - generic [ref=f4e702]:
                              - generic [ref=f4e703]: Maria Kalliopi
                              - generic [ref=f4e704]: Santorini, June
                      - listitem [ref=f4e705]:
                        - figure "Greg Halloran Greg Halloran Iceland, June" [ref=f4e707]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f4e708]: Two nights at Jökulsárlón instead of the usual one, and that was the whole difference. The second morning the ice went out on the tide and it was silent.
                          - generic [ref=f4e709]:
                            - img "Greg Halloran" [ref=f4e711]
                            - generic [ref=f4e712]:
                              - generic [ref=f4e713]: Greg Halloran
                              - generic [ref=f4e714]: Iceland, June
                - region "The questions we actually get asked" [ref=f4e715]:
                  - generic [ref=f4e716]:
                    - generic [ref=f4e717]:
                      - generic [ref=f4e718]: Before you book
                      - heading "The questions we actually get asked" [level=2] [ref=f4e720]
                      - paragraph [ref=f4e721]: If yours is not here, email hello@wanderly.example and a human will answer.
                    - generic [ref=f4e722]:
                      - group [ref=f4e723]:
                        - heading "Are flights included in the price?" [level=3] [ref=f4e725] [cursor=pointer]
                        - paragraph [ref=f4e727]: "No. Every price on this site is the land price — accommodation, guides, transport, entries and the meals listed on each itinerary. International flights are excluded on purpose: travellers join us from four continents, and quoting one fare would make the price wrong for almost everybody. We will happily hold a departure while you book yours, and we tell you which airport and which arrival window works."
                      - group [ref=f4e728]:
                        - heading "Can I customize an itinerary?" [level=3] [ref=f4e730] [cursor=pointer]
                      - group [ref=f4e731]:
                        - heading "What is the cancellation policy?" [level=3] [ref=f4e733] [cursor=pointer]
                      - group [ref=f4e734]:
                        - heading "When is the best time to visit Bali?" [level=3] [ref=f4e736] [cursor=pointer]
                      - group [ref=f4e737]:
                        - heading "How big are the groups?" [level=3] [ref=f4e739] [cursor=pointer]
                      - group [ref=f4e740]:
                        - heading "Do you charge a single supplement?" [level=3] [ref=f4e742] [cursor=pointer]
                - generic [ref=f4e745]:
                  - generic [ref=f4e746]: New itineraries, once a month
                  - paragraph [ref=f4e747]: Where we are opening next, which departures are nearly full, and the honest month-by-month verdict on each one. No offers you did not ask for.
                  - generic [ref=f4e748]:
                    - generic [ref=f4e749]: Email address
                    - textbox "Email address" [ref=f4e750]:
                      - /placeholder: you@example.com
                    - button "Keep me posted" [ref=f4e751] [cursor=pointer]
                  - paragraph [ref=f4e752]: One email a month. Unsubscribe in one click, and we do not sell the list.
                - region "This whole site is a template you can have" [ref=f4e753]:

                      - paragraph [ref=f4e760]: Every card, chart, timeline and modal on this site is a VivekUI component. Clone it, change the trips, ship it — MIT licensed, credit removable.

                - region "Six things that make a metric useful" [ref=f5e201]:
                  - generic [ref=f5e202]:
                    - generic [ref=f5e203]:
                      - generic [ref=f5e204]: Why Pulse
                      - heading "Six things that make a metric useful" [level=2] [ref=f5e206]
                      - paragraph [ref=f5e207]: A number without context is trivia. Everything here exists to put a number next to the reason it moved.
                    - list [ref=f5e208]:
                      - listitem [ref=f5e209]:
                        - generic [ref=f5e210]: ◎
                        - heading "Autocapture, then narrow" [level=3] [ref=f5e211]
                        - paragraph [ref=f5e212]: Track every click and pageview on day one, then define the twelve events that actually matter once you know what people do.
                      - listitem [ref=f5e213]:
                        - generic [ref=f5e214]: ⤸
                        - heading "Funnels that explain themselves" [level=3] [ref=f5e215]
                        - paragraph [ref=f5e216]: Every step breaks down by plan, country or campaign without leaving the chart, so "why did it drop" takes one click, not a query.
                      - listitem [ref=f5e217]:
                        - generic [ref=f5e218]: ⊞
                        - heading "Retention by cohort" [level=3] [ref=f5e219]
                        - paragraph [ref=f5e220]: Compare the month you shipped onboarding against the month before it. Three cohorts on one axis, no spreadsheet export.
                      - listitem [ref=f5e221]:
                        - generic [ref=f5e222]: ◬
                        - heading "Alerts without thresholds" [level=3] [ref=f5e223]
                        - paragraph [ref=f5e224]: Pulse learns each metric baseline and flags the spike or the drop. You set no numbers and get no 3am false positives.
                      - listitem [ref=f5e225]:
                        - generic [ref=f5e226]: ▷
                        - heading "Replay the session behind a number" [level=3] [ref=f5e227]
                        - paragraph [ref=f5e228]: Click any point on any chart to watch the sessions that produced it. The number stops being an abstraction.
                      - listitem [ref=f5e229]:
                        - generic [ref=f5e230]: ⇄
                        - heading "Your warehouse stays the source" [level=3] [ref=f5e231]
                        - paragraph [ref=f5e232]: Two-way sync with Snowflake and BigQuery. Cohorts you define in Pulse land back in your own tables.
                - region "Running quietly at scale" [ref=f5e233]:
                  - generic [ref=f5e234]:
                    - generic [ref=f5e235]:
                      - generic [ref=f5e236]: By the numbers
                      - heading "Running quietly at scale" [level=2] [ref=f5e238]
                      - paragraph [ref=f5e239]: Figures count up once when they scroll into view, and stay put under reduced-motion.
                    - generic [ref=f5e240]:

                  - generic [ref=f5e270]:
                    - generic [ref=f5e271]:
                      - generic [ref=f5e272]: Customers
                      - heading "What teams say once they have shipped with it" [level=2] [ref=f5e274]
                    - list [ref=f5e275]:
                      - listitem [ref=f5e276]:
                        - figure "Mei Lin Chen Mei Lin Chen VP Product, Aster Financial" [ref=f5e278]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f5e279]: We replaced a dashboard nobody opened with four charts everybody argues about. That is the upgrade — the arguments are now about the product instead of about the data.
                          - generic [ref=f5e280]:
                            - img "Mei Lin Chen" [ref=f5e282]
                            - generic [ref=f5e283]:
                              - generic [ref=f5e284]: Mei Lin Chen
                              - generic [ref=f5e285]: VP Product, Aster Financial
                      - listitem [ref=f5e286]:
                        - figure "Jonas Weber Jonas Weber Head of Growth, Fathom Robotics" [ref=f5e288]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f5e289]: The cohort view found our onboarding regression in about nine minutes. Our previous tool had the same data and took a fortnight and a data scientist.
                          - generic [ref=f5e290]:
                            - img "Jonas Weber" [ref=f5e292]
                            - generic [ref=f5e293]:
                              - generic [ref=f5e294]: Jonas Weber

                      - listitem [ref=f5e296]:
                        - figure "Amara Okafor Amara Okafor Staff Engineer, Northwind Labs" [ref=f5e298]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f5e299]: Anomaly alerts with no thresholds sounded like marketing. Six weeks in it has paged us twice, and both times it was right.
                          - generic [ref=f5e300]:
                            - img "Amara Okafor" [ref=f5e302]
                            - generic [ref=f5e303]:
                              - generic [ref=f5e304]: Amara Okafor

                      - heading "Three plans, no sales call to see the price" [level=2] [ref=f5e311]
                      - paragraph [ref=f5e312]: Billed monthly or yearly. Every plan includes all six chart types — they are part of the component library, not an add-on.

                      - listitem [ref=f5e314]:

                - paragraph [ref=f5e373]:
                  - text: Need the yearly discount, the feature-by-feature comparison, or the FAQ?
                  - link "See full pricing" [ref=f5e374] [cursor=pointer]:
                    - /url: /pricing

                - region "Questions about the template" [ref=f5e375]:
                  - generic [ref=f5e376]:
                    - generic [ref=f5e377]:
                      - generic [ref=f5e378]: FAQ
                      - heading "Questions about the template" [level=2] [ref=f5e380]
                    - generic [ref=f5e381]:
                      - group [ref=f5e382]:
                        - heading "Does this template include charts?" [level=3] [ref=f5e384] [cursor=pointer]
                        - paragraph [ref=f5e386]: Yes — all six of them, and no chart library is installed. The area, bar, line, pie, progress ring and sparkline charts on this site all come from @the_viveksingh/vivek-ui, which has zero runtime dependencies. They are pure SVG with no measurement step, so they render on the server and appear in the HTML before any JavaScript loads. There is no Recharts, no Chart.js and no D3 in package.json.
                      - group [ref=f5e387]:
                        - heading "Is the dashboard responsive?" [level=3] [ref=f5e389] [cursor=pointer]

                - region "Saffron House" [ref=f6e39]:
                  - img "The dining room after dark — banquettes and bare tables under low light" [ref=f6e41]
                  - generic [ref=f6e44]:
                    - generic [ref=f6e45]: Lavelle Road · Bengaluru · Since 2009
                    - heading "Saffron House" [level=1] [ref=f6e46]
                    - paragraph [ref=f6e47]: Slow-cooked Indian cooking, served under warm light.
                    - generic [ref=f6e48]:
                      - link "Reserve a table" [ref=f6e49] [cursor=pointer]:
                        - /url: /reserve
                      - link "See the menu" [ref=f6e50] [cursor=pointer]:

                - region "Our kitchen" [ref=f6e54]:
                  - generic [ref=f6e56]:
                    - img "A cook’s hands finishing a plate, tattooed forearms against a dark apron" [ref=f6e58]
                    - generic [ref=f6e59]:
                      - generic [ref=f6e60]: The kitchen
                      - heading "Sixteen years, one room, and a tandoor lit at four" [level=2] [ref=f6e61]
                      - generic [ref=f6e62]:
                        - paragraph [ref=f6e63]: Aarti Menon opened Saffron House in a converted bungalow on Lavelle Road with eleven tables and a single clay oven. Both are still here. The oven is lit at four every afternoon and does not go out until the last order is called.
                        - paragraph [ref=f6e64]: Nothing on this menu comes out of a jar. Whole spices are dry-roasted and ground each morning, the paneer is pressed in-house, and the black dal — the dish people book a month ahead for — goes on the flame the day before you eat it. It takes thirty-six hours, and there is no way to make it take less.
                      - blockquote [ref=f6e65]: “Regional food, cooked properly, at the pace it wants to be cooked. That was the whole plan. We have not needed a second one.”
                      - paragraph [ref=f6e66]: Aarti Menon, chef-owner
                - generic [ref=f6e71]:
                  - generic [ref=f6e72]:
                    - generic [ref=f6e73]: Signature dishes
                    - heading "The six we are known for" [level=2] [ref=f6e75]
                    - paragraph [ref=f6e76]: Every one of them has been on the menu since the year we opened, and none of them has changed.
                  - group "Signature dishes" [ref=f6e77]:
                    - generic [ref=f6e78]:
                      - group "Dish 1 of 6" [ref=f6e79]:
                        - generic [ref=f6e80]:
                          - img "A dark red curry in a black pan, scattered with fresh coriander" [ref=f6e82]
                          - generic [ref=f6e83]:
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e84]:
                              - generic [ref=f6e85]: Laal Maas
                              - generic [ref=f6e87]: ₹890
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e88]: Mutton on the bone in a Mathania chilli gravy, cooked down until the fat and the spice stop being separate things. Properly, unapologetically hot.
                          - generic [ref=f6e89]:
                            - generic [ref=f6e90]: Spicy
                            - generic [ref=f6e91]: Chef’s pick
                      - group "Dish 2 of 6" [ref=f6e92]:
                        - generic [ref=f6e93]:
                          - img "Biryani on a plate, mint leaves and a wedge of lime laid over the rice" [ref=f6e95]
                          - generic [ref=f6e96]:
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e97]:
                              - generic [ref=f6e98]: Kacchi Gosht Biryani
                              - generic [ref=f6e100]: ₹940
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e101]: Raw marinated mutton and aged sella rice sealed under dough and cooked once, together. Served with burani raita and mirchi ka salan. Forty minutes; worth the wait.
                          - generic [ref=f6e102]:
                            - generic [ref=f6e103]: Chef’s pick
                            - generic [ref=f6e104]: Dairy
                      - group "Dish 3 of 6" [ref=f6e105]:
                        - generic [ref=f6e106]:
                          - img "Cubes of paneer and blistered tomato in a cast-iron pan" [ref=f6e108]
                          - generic [ref=f6e109]:
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e110]:
                              - generic [ref=f6e111]: Kalonji Paneer Tikka
                              - generic [ref=f6e113]: ₹480
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e114]: Hand-pressed paneer marinated overnight in hung curd and nigella seed, charred hard on the outside and still trembling within. Served with a burnt-tomato chutney.
                          - generic [ref=f6e115]:
                            - generic [ref=f6e116]: Veg
                            - generic [ref=f6e117]: Chef’s pick
                            - generic [ref=f6e118]: Dairy
                      - group "Dish 4 of 6" [ref=f6e119]:
                        - generic [ref=f6e120]:
                          - img "Butter chicken beside two rounds of naan on a white plate" [ref=f6e122]
                          - generic [ref=f6e123]:
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e124]:
                              - generic [ref=f6e125]: Old Delhi Butter Chicken
                              - generic [ref=f6e127]: ₹760
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e128]: Tandoori thigh meat in a gravy of slow-roasted tomato, cashew and a great deal of butter. Sweet only by accident, never by design.
                          - generic [ref=f6e129]:
                            - generic [ref=f6e130]: Nuts
                            - generic [ref=f6e131]: Dairy
                      - group "Dish 5 of 6" [ref=f6e132]:
                        - generic [ref=f6e133]:

                          - generic [ref=f6e136]:
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e137]:
                              - generic [ref=f6e138]: Saffron House Dal
                              - generic [ref=f6e140]: ₹540
                            - paragraph [ref=f6e141]: Black urad simmered thirty-six hours over a low flame, finished with tomato, butter and a bruise of ginger. The dish the restaurant is named around.
                          - generic [ref=f6e142]:
                            - generic [ref=f6e143]: Veg
                            - generic [ref=f6e144]: Chef’s pick

                            - paragraph [ref=f6e155]: Seer fish in coconut milk soured with kudampuli, tempered with mustard and shallot. Ask for extra appam; everyone does.

                          - generic [ref=f6e209]:
                            - generic [ref=f6e210]: Minced lamb worked with raw papaya and twenty-one spices until it collapses at the touch of a spoon. Four to a plate, on warm ulte tawe ka paratha.

                          - generic [ref=f6e218]:
                            - generic [ref=f6e219]: Okra sliced fine and fried until it rattles, tossed in molagapodi and dried curry leaf. The most addictive thing we make, and the fastest to disappear.

                          - generic [ref=f6e229]:
                            - generic [ref=f6e230]: Tiger prawns in a dry masala of black pepper, star anise and toasted coconut. Hot in the Tamil way — aromatic first, then the heat arrives.

                      - heading "What people say afterwards" [level=2] [ref=f6e329]
                    - list [ref=f6e330]:
                      - listitem [ref=f6e331]:
                        - figure "Rhea Kulkarni Rhea Kulkarni Bengaluru" [ref=f6e333]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f6e334]: The black dal is worth the trip on its own. We arrived at nine on a Tuesday, walked straight in, and stayed until they turned the lights up.
                          - generic [ref=f6e335]:
                            - img "Rhea Kulkarni" [ref=f6e337]
                            - generic [ref=f6e338]:
                              - generic [ref=f6e339]: Rhea Kulkarni

                      - listitem [ref=f6e341]:
                        - figure "Vikram Rathore Vikram Rathore Food writer" [ref=f6e343]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f6e344]: I have eaten laal maas across Rajasthan and this one is not a compromise for city palates. They asked me twice whether I really wanted it hot. I did.
                          - generic [ref=f6e345]:
                            - img "Vikram Rathore" [ref=f6e347]
                            - generic [ref=f6e348]:
                              - generic [ref=f6e349]: Vikram Rathore
                              - generic [ref=f6e350]: Food writer
                      - listitem [ref=f6e351]:
                        - figure "Sandra Dias Sandra Dias Regular since 2016" [ref=f6e353]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f6e354]: Booked the terrace for eleven people with two days notice and they handled every dietary note without a single reminder. The kheer arrived with candles in it.
                          - generic [ref=f6e355]:
                            - img "Sandra Dias" [ref=f6e357]
                            - generic [ref=f6e358]:

                    - generic [ref=f6e370]:
                      - group [ref=f6e371]:
                        - heading "Do you take walk-ins?" [level=3] [ref=f6e373] [cursor=pointer]
                        - paragraph [ref=f6e375]: Yes. We hold about a quarter of the room back for walk-ins every service, and the bar counter is never bookable. Weekend evenings after 8 pm are the one time you should expect to wait — 30 to 45 minutes is typical. Any other night, walking in usually works.

                - region "What people said afterwards" [ref=f7e584]:
                  - generic [ref=f7e585]:
                    - generic [ref=f7e586]:
                      - generic [ref=f7e587]: Reviews
                      - heading "What people said afterwards" [level=2] [ref=f7e589]
                    - list [ref=f7e590]:
                      - listitem [ref=f7e591]:
                        - figure "Sneha Prabhu Sneha Prabhu Bought in HSR Layout" [ref=f7e593]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f7e594]: The price-trend chart is the thing I actually used. Seeing HSR go up 46% in five years told me more than any agent’s pitch, and it is on every listing page rather than buried in a report.
                          - generic [ref=f7e595]:
                            - img "Sneha Prabhu" [ref=f7e597]
                            - generic [ref=f7e598]:
                              - generic [ref=f7e599]: Sneha Prabhu
                              - generic [ref=f7e600]: Bought in HSR Layout
                      - listitem [ref=f7e601]:
                        - figure "Vikram Iyer Vikram Iyer Bought in Whitefield" [ref=f7e603]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f7e604]: I compared four flats side by side in the compare table and three of them lost on maintenance cost alone. That column does not exist on any other portal I looked at.
                          - generic [ref=f7e605]:
                            - img "Vikram Iyer" [ref=f7e607]
                            - generic [ref=f7e608]:
                              - generic [ref=f7e609]: Vikram Iyer
                              - generic [ref=f7e610]: Bought in Whitefield
                      - listitem [ref=f7e611]:
                        - figure "Fatima Sheikh Fatima Sheikh Bought in Hebbal" [ref=f7e613]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f7e614]: The EMI calculator showing interest as a slice of a pie rather than a number was quietly brutal. I dropped my tenure from twenty-five years to eighteen after looking at it.
                          - generic [ref=f7e615]:
                            - img "Fatima Sheikh" [ref=f7e617]
                            - generic [ref=f7e618]:
                              - generic [ref=f7e619]: Fatima Sheikh
                              - generic [ref=f7e620]: Bought in Hebbal
                      - listitem [ref=f7e621]:
                        - figure "Daniel Fernandes Daniel Fernandes Renting in Koramangala" [ref=f7e623]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f7e624]: Filtering by “pet friendly” and having it actually mean something on the agreement saved me three wasted viewings. Small thing, entirely the point.
                          - generic [ref=f7e625]:
                            - img "Daniel Fernandes" [ref=f7e627]
                            - generic [ref=f7e628]:
                              - generic [ref=f7e629]: Daniel Fernandes
                              - generic [ref=f7e630]: Renting in Koramangala
                - region "Questions worth answering honestly" [ref=f7e631]:
                  - generic [ref=f7e632]:
                    - generic [ref=f7e633]:
                      - generic [ref=f7e634]: FAQ
                      - heading "Questions worth answering honestly" [level=2] [ref=f7e636]
                    - generic [ref=f7e637]:
                      - group [ref=f7e638]:
                        - heading "Are these listings verified?" [level=3] [ref=f7e640] [cursor=pointer]
                        - paragraph [ref=f7e642]: No — every property, price, agent and review on NestFind is invented. This is a free open-source website template, not a working marketplace, and the eighteen Bengaluru listings exist so you can see how a real one would look before you wire up your own data. Replace data/listings.ts with your feed and the whole site follows.
                      - group [ref=f7e643]:

                      - paragraph [ref=f8e346]: "Maya has spent eleven years on the boring half of frontend: the render path, the cache, the deploy. She teaches the way she reviews code — by asking what happens on the slow connection, on the old phone, on the day the API is down. Her courses ship a real repository, not a starter kit, and every lesson ends with something running."

                - generic [ref=f8e385]:
                  - generic [ref=f8e386]:
                    - heading "What a SkillForge course actually includes" [level=2] [ref=f8e387]
                    - paragraph [ref=f8e388]: The same five things every time, so you know what you are buying before you click.
                    - list [ref=f8e389]:
                      - listitem [ref=f8e390]:
                        - progressbar [ref=f8e392]
                        - generic [ref=f8e396]:
                          - strong [ref=f8e397]: A repository you keep.
                          - text: Not a zip of finished code — the one you built, commit by commit.
                      - listitem [ref=f8e398]:
                        - progressbar [ref=f8e400]
                        - generic [ref=f8e404]:
                          - strong [ref=f8e405]: Projects, not exercises.
                          - text: A third to a half of every course is hands-on, and the split is shown on each course page.
                      - listitem [ref=f8e406]:
                        - progressbar [ref=f8e408]
                        - generic [ref=f8e412]:
                          - strong [ref=f8e413]: Lifetime access, including updates.
                          - text: Instructors re-record when the tooling moves; you get the revision free.
                      - listitem [ref=f8e414]:
                        - progressbar [ref=f8e416]
                        - generic [ref=f8e420]:
                          - strong [ref=f8e421]: A certificate with a verification link.
                          - text: On free courses too.
                      - listitem [ref=f8e422]:
                        - progressbar [ref=f8e424]
                        - generic [ref=f8e428]:
                          - strong [ref=f8e429]: Thirty days to change your mind.
                          - text: No questions, and you keep the certificate.
                  - generic [ref=f8e432]:
                    - progressbar "Average course completion rate" [ref=f8e433]:
                      - generic:
                        - generic:
                          - generic: 68%
                          - generic: finish rate
                    - paragraph [ref=f8e437]: Most online courses are abandoned around lesson three. Ours are finished because each one is building a specific thing, and stopping halfway leaves it broken.
                - region "What people said afterwards" [ref=f8e438]:
                  - generic [ref=f8e439]:
                    - generic [ref=f8e440]:
                      - generic [ref=f8e441]: Learners
                      - heading "What people said afterwards" [level=2] [ref=f8e443]
                    - list [ref=f8e444]:
                      - listitem [ref=f8e445]:
                        - figure "Priya Nandakumar Priya Nandakumar Frontend engineer, Meridian" [ref=f8e447]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f8e448]: I had been "learning React" for two years and had nothing to show anyone. Six weeks here and I have a deployed app with real users. The difference is that every lesson ends with something running.
                          - generic [ref=f8e449]:
                            - img "Priya Nandakumar" [ref=f8e451]
                            - generic [ref=f8e452]:
                              - generic [ref=f8e453]: Priya Nandakumar
                              - generic [ref=f8e454]: Frontend engineer, Meridian
                      - listitem [ref=f8e455]:
                        - figure "Clara Bergström Clara Bergström Product engineer, Fathom" [ref=f8e457]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f8e458]: The Postgres course paid for itself in one afternoon. I found an index we had been missing for a year and cut our slowest endpoint from nine seconds to under two hundred milliseconds.
                          - generic [ref=f8e459]:
                            - img "Clara Bergström" [ref=f8e461]
                            - generic [ref=f8e462]:
                              - generic [ref=f8e463]: Clara Bergström
                              - generic [ref=f8e464]: Product engineer, Fathom
                      - listitem [ref=f8e465]:
                        - figure "Peter Nkemelu Peter Nkemelu Engineering manager, Halcyon" [ref=f8e467]:
                          - blockquote [ref=f8e468]: We put four engineers through the delivery course. CI went from twenty-two minutes to five, and we now rehearse rollbacks monthly. That was not on the syllabus — it came out of the projects.
                          - generic [ref=f8e469]:
                            - img "Peter Nkemelu" [ref=f8e471]
                            - generic [ref=f8e472]:
                              - generic [ref=f8e473]: Peter Nkemelu
                              - generic [ref=f8e474]: Engineering manager, Halcyon
                - region "Pay per course, or take the lot" [ref=f8e475]:
                  - generic [ref=f8e476]:

                      - generic [ref=f9e207]: "05"
                      - generic [ref=f9e208]:
                        - heading "Six ways to train" [level=2] [ref=f9e209]
                        - paragraph [ref=f9e210]: Every programme runs at least twice a week, and every membership above Basic includes all of them.
                    - list [ref=f9e212]:
                      - listitem [ref=f9e213]:
                        - heading "Strength" [level=3] [ref=f9e218]
                        - paragraph [ref=f9e219]: Barbell work on a five-week wave. Squat, press, hinge, and one logbook you keep from your first session onward.
                      - listitem [ref=f9e220]:
                        - heading "HIIT" [level=3] [ref=f9e224]
                        - paragraph [ref=f9e225]: Forty-five minutes of intervals against a clock that does not negotiate. The highest-output class on the board.
                      - listitem [ref=f9e226]:
                        - heading "Yoga" [level=3] [ref=f9e232]
                        - paragraph [ref=f9e233]: Slow, warm and unhurried. Mostly hips and thoracic spine, because that is what lifting takes out of you.
                      - listitem [ref=f9e234]:
                        - heading "CrossFit" [level=3] [ref=f9e240]
                        - paragraph [ref=f9e241]: A scored session every time, written up three ways before the doors open so nobody has to ask for the easier one.
                      - listitem [ref=f9e242]:
                        - heading "Boxing" [level=3] [ref=f9e248]
                        - paragraph [ref=f9e249]: Pads, bags and footwork. Technical rounds first, conditioning last, and no sparring unless you ask for it.
                      - listitem [ref=f9e250]:
                        - heading "Mobility" [level=3] [ref=f9e256]
                        - paragraph [ref=f9e257]: Thirty minutes of end-range work and breathing. Booked after a first heavy week, and then never dropped.
                    - link "All six classes in detail" [ref=f9e259] [cursor=pointer]:

                      - paragraph [ref=f10e377]: Web and AI take the largest share of the 48-session programme. Career is the smallest track but runs in the largest hall — it fills up first.

                      - heading "How to plan your two days" [level=3] [ref=f10e380]
                      - paragraph [ref=f10e381]: Every talk is recorded, so do not spend the day picking between two rooms — go to the one with the speaker you want to corner afterwards, and watch the other next week.
                      - paragraph [ref=f10e382]: Workshops are the exception. They are capped at forty seats, not recorded, and the seat is the value. Book them first.

                        - paragraph [ref=f10e844]: Full refund up to 30 days before the event, no questions asked. Between 30 and 7 days you get 50% back, or a free name change to a colleague.                 - generic [ref=f11e77]:
                  - article [ref=f11e78]:
                    - link [ref=f11e79] [cursor=pointer]:
                      - /url: /article/the-numbers-behind-indias-developer-boom
                      - img "A city street at night seen from above, lit by traffic and shopfronts" [ref=f11e80]
                    - generic [ref=f11e81]:
                      - generic [ref=f11e82]:
                        - generic [ref=f11e83]: Data
                        - generic [ref=f11e84]: The lead
                      - heading [level=2] [ref=f11e85]:
                        - link "The numbers behind India’s developer boom" [ref=f11e86] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: /article/the-numbers-behind-indias-developer-boom
                      - paragraph [ref=f11e87]: India is on course to host the largest developer population on earth before 2029. The headline is the least interesting part of the chart.
                      - generic [ref=f11e88]:
                        - img "Ananya Rao" [ref=f11e90]
                        - generic [ref=f11e91]:
                          - text: Ananya Rao ·
                          - time [ref=f11e92]: last week
                          - text: · 7 min read
                  - generic [ref=f11e93]:
                    - region [ref=f11e94]:
                      - generic [ref=f11e95]:
                        - heading "Latest" [level=2] [ref=f11e96]
                        - generic [ref=f11e97]: Updated continuously
                      - generic [ref=f11e98]:
                        - generic [ref=f11e99]:
                          - img "A dark globe threaded with glowing network connections" [ref=f11e100]
                          - generic [ref=f11e101]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e102]: Dispatches
                            - generic [ref=f11e103]: 6 min
                          - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e104]:
                            - link "Night shift at the data centre" [ref=f11e105] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /article/night-shift-at-the-data-centre
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e106]: Between midnight and six, the cloud is eleven people, a golf cart and a very specific smell.
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e107]:
                            - text: Zainab Qureshi ·
                            - time [ref=f11e108]: last week
                        - generic [ref=f11e109]:
                          - img "A group of people working and talking around a shared table" [ref=f11e110]
                          - generic [ref=f11e111]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e112]: Culture
                            - generic [ref=f11e113]: 5 min
                          - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e114]:
                            - link "The group chat is the last good website" [ref=f11e115] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /article/the-group-chat-is-the-last-good-website
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e116]: The open web got optimised into a shopping mall. Everything worth reading migrated into rooms of eleven people.
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e117]:
                            - text: Devika Menon ·
                            - time [ref=f11e118]: last week
                        - generic [ref=f11e119]:
                          - img "A dense circuit board photographed close up, its traces forming a grid" [ref=f11e120]
                          - generic [ref=f11e121]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e122]: Systems
                            - generic [ref=f11e123]: 6 min
                          - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e124]:
                            - link "The quiet return of the monolith" [ref=f11e125] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /article/the-quiet-return-of-the-monolith
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e126]: Nobody announced it. Teams simply started merging services back together and hoping their old conference talks would age gracefully.
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e127]:
                            - text: Rohan Bhatt ·
                            - time [ref=f11e128]: last week
                        - generic [ref=f11e129]:
                          - img "Rows of old books receding down a library shelf" [ref=f11e130]
                          - generic [ref=f11e131]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e132]: Data
                            - generic [ref=f11e133]: 6 min
                          - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e134]:
                            - link "How long does a link live?" [ref=f11e135] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /article/how-long-does-a-link-live
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e136]: We resolved 41,000 URLs cited in technology writing between 2010 and 2024. A quarter of them are already gone.
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e137]:
                            - text: Tomás Oliveira ·
                            - time [ref=f11e138]: 2 weeks ago
                        - generic [ref=f11e139]:
                          - img "Lines of colourful source code displayed on a dark monitor" [ref=f11e140]
                          - generic [ref=f11e141]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e142]: Systems
                            - generic [ref=f11e143]: 5 min
                          - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e144]:
                            - link "What we lost when we stopped writing CSS" [ref=f11e145] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /article/what-we-lost-when-we-stopped-writing-css
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e146]: A generation of engineers can ship a design system and cannot centre a div. That is not a skills problem. It is a tooling decision that hardened into a worldview.
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e147]:
                            - text: Meera Krishnan ·
                            - time [ref=f11e148]: 2 weeks ago
                        - generic [ref=f11e149]:
                          - img "An old printing press with ink rollers and metal frames" [ref=f11e150]
                          - generic [ref=f11e151]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e152]: Dispatches
                            - generic [ref=f11e153]: 5 min
                          - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e154]:
                            - link "The last letterpress in Fort Kochi" [ref=f11e155] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /article/the-last-letterpress-in-fort-kochi
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e156]: K. R. Damodaran has been setting type by hand since 1971. His customers are now almost entirely people who design on screens.
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e157]:
                            - text: Devika Menon ·
                            - time [ref=f11e158]: 2 weeks ago
                        - generic [ref=f11e159]:
                          - img "A stack of folded newspapers photographed from the side" [ref=f11e160]
                          - generic [ref=f11e161]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e162]: Culture
                            - generic [ref=f11e163]: 6 min
                          - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e164]:
                            - link "Everyone is a publisher. Nobody is an editor." [ref=f11e165] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /article/everyone-is-a-publisher-nobody-is-an-editor
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e166]: Publishing became free and editing did not. Two decades on, we can see exactly what that asymmetry produced.
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e167]:
                            - text: Ananya Rao ·
                            - time [ref=f11e168]: 2 weeks ago
                        - generic [ref=f11e169]:
                          - img "A long exposure of a road at dusk with mountains behind" [ref=f11e170]
                          - generic [ref=f11e171]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e172]: Data
                            - generic [ref=f11e173]: 5 min
                          - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e174]:
                            - link "The commute came back" [ref=f11e175] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /article/the-commute-came-back
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e176]: Office attendance in nine Indian cities has climbed for eleven straight quarters. The story underneath is about who could never work from home in the first place.
                          - paragraph [ref=f11e177]:
                            - text: Zainab Qureshi ·
                            - time [ref=f11e178]: 3 weeks ago
                    - complementary [ref=f11e179]:
                      - region [ref=f11e180]:

                      - tabpanel "Systems" [ref=f11e399]:
                        - paragraph [ref=f11e400]: How software is actually built, and what it costs the people who maintain it.
                        - generic [ref=f11e401]:
                          - generic [ref=f11e402]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e403]:
                              - generic [ref=f11e404]: Systems
                              - generic [ref=f11e405]: 6 min
                            - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e406]:
                              - link "The quiet return of the monolith" [ref=f11e407] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /article/the-quiet-return-of-the-monolith
                            - paragraph [ref=f11e408]: Nobody announced it. Teams simply started merging services back together and hoping their old conference talks would age gracefully.
                            - paragraph [ref=f11e409]:
                              - text: Rohan Bhatt ·
                              - time [ref=f11e410]: last week
                          - generic [ref=f11e411]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e412]:
                              - generic [ref=f11e413]: Systems
                              - generic [ref=f11e414]: 5 min
                            - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e415]:
                              - link "What we lost when we stopped writing CSS" [ref=f11e416] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /article/what-we-lost-when-we-stopped-writing-css
                            - paragraph [ref=f11e417]: A generation of engineers can ship a design system and cannot centre a div. That is not a skills problem. It is a tooling decision that hardened into a worldview.
                            - paragraph [ref=f11e418]:
                              - text: Meera Krishnan ·
                              - time [ref=f11e419]: 2 weeks ago
                          - generic [ref=f11e420]:
                            - generic [ref=f11e421]:
                              - generic [ref=f11e422]: Systems
                              - generic [ref=f11e423]: 5 min
                            - heading [level=3] [ref=f11e424]:
                              - link "The machine that forgets" [ref=f11e425] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /article/the-machine-that-forgets
                            - paragraph [ref=f11e426]: Cache invalidation is called one of the two hard problems as a joke. Six outages later, the joke stops being funny.

                              - text: Rohan Bhatt ·

              - main [ref=f12e39]:
                - region "Ship a chat UI before lunch" [ref=f12e41]:
                  - generic [ref=f12e43]:
                    - generic [ref=f12e44]:
                      - generic [ref=f12e45]: Free & open source · MIT
                      - heading "Ship a chat UI before lunch" [level=1] [ref=f12e47]
                      - generic [ref=f12e48]:
                        - text: The chat panel beside this text is not a screenshot — it is
                        - strong [ref=f12e49]: ChatThread
                        - text: ","
                        - strong [ref=f12e50]: ChatMessage
                        - text: ","
                        - strong [ref=f12e51]: TypingIndicator
                        - text: and
                        - strong [ref=f12e52]: ChatCodeBlock
                        - text: running live, from a component library with zero runtime dependencies. Fork the template, point it at your model, ship.
                      - generic [ref=f12e53]:
                        - link "Try the demo" [ref=f12e54] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: /chat
                        - link "Read the docs" [ref=f12e55] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - /url: https://ui.vivekkumarsingh.in/docs?utm_source=vivekui-template&utm_campaign=aichat&utm_medium=hero
                    - generic [ref=f12e57]:
                      - generic [ref=f12e58]:
                        - generic [ref=f12e63]: chatterbox — live components, not a screenshot
                        - button "Pause the demo" [ref=f12e64] [cursor=pointer]:
                          - generic [ref=f12e65]: ❙❙
                      - log "Scripted demo conversation" [ref=f12e68]:
                        - article "You" [ref=f12e69]:
                          - img "You" [ref=f12e71]:
                            - generic [ref=f12e72]: "Y"
                          - generic [ref=f12e73]:
                            - generic [ref=f12e74]: You
                            - generic [ref=f12e76]: Give me the React for a chat thread with a typing indicator.
                        - article "ChatterBox" [ref=f12e77]:
                          - img "ChatterBox" [ref=f12e79]:
                            - generic [ref=f12e80]: CB
                          - generic [ref=f12e81]:
                            - generic [ref=f12e82]: ChatterBox
                            - generic [ref=f12e84]:
                              - paragraph [ref=f12e85]: "Two components. The thread owns scrolling and the live region:"
                              - generic [ref=f12e86]:
                                - generic [ref=f12e87]:
                                  - generic [ref=f12e88]: components/chat.tsx
                                  - generic [ref=f12e89]: tsx
                                  - button "Copy code" [ref=f12e90] [cursor=pointer]:
                                    - generic [ref=f12e91]: ⧉
                                - group "components/chat.tsx" [ref=f12e93]:
                                  - code [ref=f12e94]: "import { ChatThread, ChatInput } from '@the_viveksingh/vivek-ui' <ChatThread messages={messages} loading={pending} /> <ChatInput onSubmit={send} placeholder=\"Ask anything…\" />"
                                - status [ref=f12e95]
                - region "The parts of a chat UI nobody wants to build twice" [ref=f12e97]:
                  - generic [ref=f12e98]:
                    - generic [ref=f12e99]:
                      - generic [ref=f12e100]: What you get
                      - heading "The parts of a chat UI nobody wants to build twice" [level=2] [ref=f12e102]
                      - paragraph [ref=f12e103]: Five components cover the whole surface. None of them ships a dependency, and most render on the server.
                    - list [ref=f12e104]:
                      - listitem [ref=f12e105]:
                        - generic [ref=f12e106]: ▶
                        - heading "Streaming-shaped rendering" [level=3] [ref=f12e107]
                        - paragraph [ref=f12e108]: A typing indicator that announces itself once instead of on every token, and auto-scroll that sticks to the bottom only while the reader is already there. Push tokens onto the last message; the thread does the rest.
                      - listitem [ref=f12e109]:
                        - generic [ref=f12e110]: ⌘
                        - heading "Code blocks that copy" [level=3] [ref=f12e111]
                        - paragraph [ref=f12e112]: Fenced blocks with a filename header, a copy button whose accessible name never changes, and horizontal scroll a keyboard user can actually reach. Bring your own highlighter through one render prop.
                      - listitem [ref=f12e113]:
                        - generic [ref=f12e114]: ▤
                        - heading "Charts inside the bubble" [level=3] [ref=f12e115]
                        - paragraph [ref=f12e116]: Six SVG chart types at a separate subpath. Drop a BarChart into a message and it renders as part of the answer — no canvas, no d3, no second package.
                      - listitem [ref=f12e117]:
                        - generic [ref=f12e118]: ◐
                        - heading "Theming by custom property" [level=3] [ref=f12e119]
                        - paragraph [ref=f12e120]: Every colour, radius and font is a CSS variable. The emerald on this page is four declarations. Dark mode is one attribute on <html>, and it survives JavaScript being switched off.
                      - listitem [ref=f12e121]:
                        - generic [ref=f12e122]: ✓
                        - heading "Accessible by construction" [level=3] [ref=f12e123]

                        - paragraph [ref=f12e387]: "Yes. ChatterBox is UI only — there is no model, no SDK and no API key anywhere in it. The components take messages in and render them, so they sit in front of whatever you already run: Anthropic, OpenAI, Mistral, or an open-weights model on your own hardware. Wire ChatInput’s onSubmit to a server action or a route handler and append tokens to the last message as they arrive."
```

# Test source

```ts
  1   | import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test'
  2   | import { axeViolations, KEY_ROUTES } from './helpers'
  3   | 
  4   | /**
  5   |  * axe against whole pages, in a real browser.
  6   |  *
  7   |  * The unit suite already runs axe on every component — but in isolation, in jsdom. That
  8   |  * misses everything that only exists once a page is assembled, and everything that needs
  9   |  * layout to detect:
  10  |  *
  11  |  *   - two landmarks of the same kind with no distinguishing label
  12  |  *   - a second `<h1>`, or an outline that jumps a level, once sections are composed
  13  |  *   - **colour contrast**, which axe skips entirely in jsdom because it has to sample the
  14  |  *     rendered pixels of an element against whatever is actually painted behind it
  15  |  *
  16  |  * That last one is the reason this file earns its keep. Every contrast claim the library
  17  |  * makes has been checked against token pairs in the abstract; this is the first thing that
  18  |  * checks the colours as a browser really paints them, on a real background, at a real
  19  |  * font size.
  20  |  */
  21  | 
  22  | /*
  23  |  * Rules disabled, with the reason each one is not ours to fix.
  24  |  *
  25  |  * Nothing to do with severity — an unexplained disabled rule is how an accessibility suite
  26  |  * quietly stops testing anything.
  27  |  */
  28  | const NOT_OURS = [
  29  |   // The showcase embeds twelve third-party sites. axe walks into same-origin frames and
  30  |   // reports their problems as ours; we cannot fix another site's markup from here.
  31  |   'frame-tested',
  32  | ]
  33  | 
  34  | test.describe('accessibility of the composed pages', () => {
  35  |   for (const route of KEY_ROUTES) {
```
